# Chapter 1 — Reunion at a familiar house

## Taglish editing copy

Exported from the current game on 4 October 2026. Includes every branch of the currently playable Chapter 1.

This is an editing copy; changing it does not change the game automatically. Keep scene and line IDs when editing so revisions can be matched reliably. Scene sections follow source order; use the “Next” and choice destinations to follow a particular route. Alternative branches are not all played in a single run.

Narration has no speaker label. MC’s silent thoughts are italicized. Spoken dialogue has a speaker label. Bracketed notes are implementation directions. Placeholders such as {name} remain for player customization. English narration shared by both modes is intentionally repeated. These are the translations currently used by the game, including any existing wording issues or fallback text, rather than a newly rewritten translation.

## 01 / The road back [journey]

[Background: bus; time: day.]

[Audio direction: "bus".]

[Existing CG: way-back. Begins at the line containing “The sea appears”; ends at the line containing “SAINT LUIS!”.]

[journey:0]

It’s been eight hours since we left the city, including the stopover. I can’t get comfortable anymore.

[journey:1]

I move my knee away from the seat in front of me. The window keeps rattling beside my head.

[journey:2]

I grew up in Saint Luis with Lola nearby.

[journey:3]

My parents took me to Manila when I was eleven.

[journey:4]

I kept hoping I’d come back the next year. I never did.

[journey:5]

Twelve years have passed. I’m twenty-three now.

[journey:6]

The sea appears between two buildings. I lean closer to the window to get a better look.

[journey:7]

*I’m finally back in Saint Luis.*

[journey:8]

There are small fishing boats in the bay. I can see the old baywalk along the shore.

[journey:9]

Lola used to take Rowan and me for walks there in the afternoons.

[journey:10]

She held our hands when we crossed the road. Me on one side, Rowan on the other.

[journey:11]

He’d complain that we weren’t little anymore. Then keep holding on after we’d crossed.

[journey:12]

**MC:** Hay. I wonder how he’s been.

[journey:13]

Thinking about him makes me smile.

[journey:14]

Then I remember Lola’s funeral.

[journey:15]

*He was probably at the funeral.*

[journey:16]

Two years ago, Lola died during finals week.

[journey:17]

My parents said I couldn’t miss my exams.

[journey:18]

I wanted to argue. Instead, I asked if we could go afterward.

[journey:19]

“We’ll talk about it.”

[journey:20]

We never did.

[journey:21]

I still took the exams. Handed everything in on time.

[journey:22]

*I was her grandchild, and I wasn’t there.*

[journey:23]

This trip is different.

[journey:24]

I paid for the ticket with money I’d saved.

[journey:25]

I told my parents I was leaving. I didn’t ask.

[journey:26]

Then I left before we could finish another argument.

[journey:27]

I needed a break from college. From home. From having the same conversation in my head.

[journey:28]

Lola’s house needs cleaning. A few things probably need fixing.

[journey:29]

*I can do that.*

[journey:30]

*Sweep a floor. Fix a window. Get some sleep.*

[journey:31]

*That was the plan.*

[journey:32]

I look back toward the baywalk.

[journey:33]

*What am I going to say if I see Rowan?*

[journey:34]

*Sorry I stopped calling?*

[journey:35]

*Sorry I believed you’d forgotten me?*

[journey:36]

**Conductor:** SAINT LUIS! SAINT LUIS!

[journey:37]

The shout makes me jump. It wakes the passenger across the aisle too.

[journey:38]

**MC:** Grabe.

[journey:39]

*At least I’m not the only one he scared.*

**Next:** busBump.

## A very good start [busBump]

[Background: bus; time: day.]

[Audio direction: "bus".]

[busBump:0]

Everyone gets up at once. After eight hours, we’re all ready to get off this bus.

[busBump:1]

I reach for my bag.

[busBump:2]

A man bumps into me. My hand slips off the seat, and I fall into the aisle.

[busBump:3]

**MC:** Aray!

[busBump:4]

I sit on the floor for a moment. My hip hurts, and my bag is still caught around my wrist.

[busBump:5]

**Stranger:** Sorry! Pababa na kasi ’yung anak ko.

[busBump:6]

He looks toward the front of the bus.

[busBump:7]

**Stranger:** Kailangan ko siyang habulin.

**Next:** wallet.

## A very good start [wallet]

[Background: bus; time: day.]

[Audio direction: "bus".]

[wallet:0]

I brush the dirt off my clothes.

[wallet:1]

**MC:** Ang ganda ng simula.

[wallet:2]

I check that I still have my bag and phone. Then I reach for my wallet.

[wallet:3]

I check both back pockets.

[wallet:4]

They’re empty.

[wallet:5]

I search the seat, the floor underneath it, and the aisle.

[wallet:6]

**MC:** Hala. Please naman.

[wallet:7]

My wallet is gone.

[wallet:8]

I look toward the exit. The man is gone too.

[wallet:9]

*Did he take it when he bumped into me? Was there even a child with him?*

[wallet:10]

**MC:** Shit.

[wallet:11]

My cards and IDs are in my bag. I check them twice anyway.

[wallet:12]

The wallet only had cash.

[wallet:13]

But I needed that cash to pay for the ride to Lola’s house.

[wallet:14]

Through the windshield, I can see the road toward town.

[wallet:15]

The nearest ATM is in town, too far to walk with my bag. Lola’s house is the other way.

[wallet:16]

**MC:** Napakamalas naman.

[wallet:17]

I hold onto my bag and try to think of what to do.

[wallet:18]

Then I remember that some buses pass through Santiago Street, near Lola’s house.

[wallet:19]

*Maybe this one does. I should ask.*

**Next:** driver.

## A small kindness [driver]

[Background: bus; time: day.]

[Audio direction: "bus".]

[driver:0]

**MC:** Manong? Excuse me po.

[driver:1]

The driver looks over.

[driver:2]

**MC:** Dadaan po ba kayo sa Santiago Street?

[driver:3]

**Driver:** Oo. Doon ka bababa?

[driver:4]

**MC:** Opo.

[driver:5]

I feel a little better. At least the bus goes where I need to go.

[driver:6]

**MC:** Thank you po.

[driver:7]

Then I notice the tickets in the conductor’s hand.

[driver:8]

*Will I have to pay extra to stay on? I don’t have any cash left.*

[driver:9]

I briefly think about getting off before they ask me to pay. But then I’d still be stuck here.

[driver:10]

**MC:** May dagdag po ba? Kasi—

[driver:11]

**Driver:** Upo ka na. Hindi na kita sisingilin.

[driver:12]

Before I can explain, he points toward where I fell.

[driver:13]

**Driver:** Nakita kong nabangga ka. May nawala?

[driver:14]

**MC:** Wallet ko po.

[driver:15]

The driver sighs.

[driver:16]

**Driver:** Hay. Sige. Sa Santiago kita ibababa.

[driver:17]

**MC:** Salamat po. Talaga.

[driver:18]

He points to an empty seat.

[driver:19]

**Driver:** Hawakang mabuti ’yang bag mo.

[driver:20]

**MC:** Opo.

[driver:21]

I sit down and keep my bag on my lap as the bus starts moving again.

[driver:22]

I watch the houses pass by the window.

[driver:23]

I’m still upset about my wallet.

[driver:24]

But I don’t have to figure out the rest of the trip standing in the aisle.

[driver:25]

*For now, I can sit.*

**Next:** hill.

## The hill [hill]

[Background: saint-luis; time: day.]

[Audio direction: "home".]

[Existing CG: hill-houses. Begins at the line containing “I can see his house”; ends according to the scene transition.]

[hill:0]

**MC:** Dito na po. Thank you!

[hill:1]

**Driver:** Ingat.

[hill:2]

I wait until the bus has gone. Then I turn toward the hill.

[hill:3]

It looks smaller.

[hill:4]

My legs disagree almost immediately.

[hill:5]

Halfway up, I have to change hands with my bag.

[hill:6]

Rowan and I used to race here. Downhill was easy.

[hill:7]

Uphill, one of us always started arguing about where the finish line was.

[hill:8]

I can see his house now. And beyond it, Lola’s porch.

[hill:9]

After we’d worn ourselves out, she’d call us in for bibingka.

[hill:10]

We’d eat before it had cooled properly. Then go straight back to hide-and-seek.

[hill:11]

I remember those afternoons so clearly.

[hill:12]

The heat. The crumbs. Trying not to laugh when Rowan walked past my hiding place.

[hill:13]

*I can’t remember the last thing I said to her.*

[hill:14]

My grip tightens around the bag. I try.

[hill:15]

*Something ordinary, probably.*

[hill:16]

*Something I thought I’d get to follow up on.*

[hill:17]

The path blurs.

[hillCry:6]

[Audio direction: "home".]

[hillHold:1]

*Not here.*

[hillHold:2]

## The other door [otherDoor]

[Background: saint-luis; time: day.]

[Audio direction: "home".]

[otherDoor:0]

*The house won’t clean itself.*

[otherDoor:1]

That sounds like something Lola would have said. I almost laugh.

[otherDoor:2]

Rowan’s house is right there.

[otherDoor:3]

The nameplate says Mayumi. The paint around it looks newer than the rest of the gate.

[otherDoor:4]

*I could knock. Just to let them know I’m back.*

[otherDoor:5]

*That would be normal. Polite, even.*

[otherDoor:6]

I rehearse it.

[otherDoor:7]

*Hi. It’s been a while.*

[otherDoor:8]

*Twelve years is probably longer than “a while.”*

[otherDoor:9]

*What if he opens the door?*

[otherDoor:10]

*What if he looks happy to see me?*

[otherDoor:11]

*Somehow, I haven’t prepared for that either.*

[otherDoor:12]

**MC:** Kaya ko namang mag-hello.

[otherDoor:13]

**MC:** In theory.

## The other door [neighborVisit]

[Background: saint-luis; time: day.]

[Audio direction: "home".]

[neighborVisit:0]

I turn toward the porch before I can hold a full debate about it.

[neighborVisit:1]

Up close, the door is familiar enough to make my stomach tighten.

[neighborVisit:2]

*How many afternoons did I spend waiting here?*

[neighborVisit:3]

I knock.

[neighborVisit:4]

Nothing. I listen.

[neighborVisit:5]

Then knock again, a little louder.

[neighborVisit:6]

**MC:** Tita Mayumi? Hello?

[neighborVisit:7]

I hesitate before calling for him.

[neighborVisit:8]

**MC:** Rowan?

[neighborVisit:9]

I wait longer than I need to.

[neighborVisit:10]

**MC:** Okay. Walang tao.

[neighborVisit:11]

The relief is immediate. The disappointment takes another second.

[neighborVisit:12]

I step off the porch and head toward Lola’s house.

## The other door [neighborWait]

[Background: saint-luis; time: day.]

[Audio direction: "home".]

[neighborWait:0]

I keep walking.

[neighborWait:1]

*I’ve been traveling all day. I’ve lost my money. I’m not sure what my face is doing.*

[neighborWait:2]

*I can put my bag down before trying to explain twelve years.*

[neighborWait:3]

[neighborWait:5]

## A familiar stranger [doorRepair]

[Background: repair; time: day.]

[Audio direction: "quiet".]

[doorRepair:0]

I stop.

[doorRepair:1]

Another tap. Then a scrape.

[doorRepair:2]

Someone’s at the house.

[doorRepair:3]

**MC:** What the hell?

[doorRepair:4]

I hurry the last few steps.

[doorRepair:5]

A man about my age is kneeling in front of the door, his back toward me.

[doorRepair:6]

A screwdriver lies beside his knee. He has a hammer in one hand.

[doorRepair:7]

The door is open just far enough that I can’t see what he’s doing to it.

[doorRepair:8]

My bag knocks against my leg as I climb onto the porch.

[doorRepair:9]

**MC:** Excuse me!

[doorRepair:10]

He stops.

[doorRepair:11]

**MC:** Anong ginagawa mo? Bahay ng lola ko ’yan.

[doorRepair:12]

The hammer lowers.

[doorRepair:13]

The man turns.

**Next:** recognition.

[Background: reunion-porch; time: day.]

[Music: reunion.]

## A little too much [rEmotion]

[Background: reunion-porch; time: day.]

[Music: reunion.]

[Rowan sprite: adult; CGs take precedence while visible.]

[rCry:5]

[rCry:6]

[rCry:7]

[rCry:8]

He waits.

[rCry:9]

[rCry:10]

[rCry:11 · Only if reunionBoundary = "hug"]

[rCry:12 · Only if reunionBoundary = "space"]

## Two years earlier [rBedroom]

[Background: manila-bedroom; time: night.]

[Music: rainy-night.]

[Rowan sprite: hidden; CGs take precedence while visible.]

[rBedroom:0]

Lola had died a few days earlier.

[rBedroom:1]

I was in my room, staring at my phone.

[rBedroom:2]

I kept opening her contact.

[rBedroom:3]

There was no reason to. I knew she wouldn’t answer.

[rBedroom:4]

Downstairs, my parents were talking.

[rBedroom:5]

They sounded normal.

[rBedroom:6]

I knew people didn’t have to cry all the time to be grieving.

[rBedroom:7]

*Still, I couldn’t understand how they could sound so normal.*

[rBedroom:8]

*I wanted to go back to Saint Luis.*

[rBedroom:9]

*Even if I was already too late for the funeral.*

[rBedroom:10]

I stood up before I could talk myself out of asking.

**Next:** rLanding.

[rBurning:8]

I go stiff.

## The yellow envelope [rEnvelope]

[Background: manila-bedroom; time: night.]

[Music: letters.]

[Rowan sprite: hidden; CGs take precedence while visible.]

[rEnvelope:0]

I close my bedroom door.

[rEnvelope:1]

For a while, I stand with my hand on the handle.

[rEnvelope:2]

Then I look down.

[rEnvelope:3]

The envelope is bent where I’ve been gripping it.

[rEnvelope:4]

Yellow paper. My name.

[rEnvelope:5]

A little boat beneath his.

[rEnvelope:6]

I sit on the bed and turn it over.

[rEnvelope:7]

It’s still sealed.

## Not tonight [rKeepSealed]

[Background: manila-bedroom; time: night.]

[Music: letters.]

[Rowan sprite: hidden; CGs take precedence while visible.]

[rKeepSealed:0]

I put my finger under the edge of the flap.

[rKeepSealed:1]

Then pull it away.

[rKeepSealed:2]

*I want to read it.*

[rKeepSealed:3]

*I just can’t do it while I can still smell the smoke on my clothes.*

[rKeepSealed:4]

**MC:** Huwag ngayon.

[rKeepSealed:5]

I tuck the envelope inside a notebook and slide it into my bag.

[rKeepSealed:6]

*They aren’t taking this one.*

[rKeepSealed:7]

I keep it.

[rKeepSealed:8]

Through the next semester. Through changing bags. Through every time I think about finding him.

[rKeepSealed:9]

After a while, opening it feels like another thing I’ve waited too long to do.

[rKeepSealed:10]

But when I pack for Saint Luis, I bring it.

**Next:** rAfterEnvelope.

[Background: manila-bedroom; time: night.]

[Music: letters.]

[Rowan sprite: hidden; CGs take precedence while visible.]

[rReadNowAfter:2]

[rReadNowAfter:3]

*Still asking him to reach things.*

[rReadNowAfter:4]

*Still sending reminders through someone else’s letter.*

[rReadNowAfter:5]

I read the part about her twice.

[rReadNowAfter:6]

Then I fold the pages along the same creases.

[rReadNowAfter:7]

*He was waiting for an answer.*

[rReadNowAfter:8]

*They both were.*

[rReadNowAfter:9]

**MC:** Na-miss din kita.

[rReadNowAfter:10]

There’s nobody here to hear it.

[rReadNowAfter:11]

I put the letter back in its envelope and keep it beside me that night.

**Next:** rAfterEnvelope.

## What I carried [rAfterEnvelope]

[Background: manila-bedroom; time: night.]

[Music: letters.]

[Rowan sprite: hidden; CGs take precedence while visible.]

[rAfterEnvelope:0]

After that, whenever I thought about contacting Rowan, I remembered the box.

[rAfterEnvelope:1]

I was still living with my parents. I was afraid of what else they might take away.

[rAfterEnvelope:2]

And I didn’t know how to explain what had happened.

[rAfterEnvelope:3]

So I kept putting it off.

## Familiar, and different [rInside]

[Background: living; time: day.]

[Music: back-together.]

[Rowan sprite: hidden; CGs take precedence while visible.]

[rInside:0]

Rowan pushes the door open.

[rInside:1]

It moves without catching.

[rInside:2]

I follow him in.

[rInside:3]

The curtains are clean. The table has been wiped down.

[rInside:4]

Lola’s old chair is still by the window.

[rInside:5]

There’s a new piece of wood beneath one armrest. It’s lighter than the rest.

[rInside:6]

**MC:** Inayos mo ’yung upuan niya.

[rInside:7]

**Rowan:** Lumuwag kasi.

[rInside:8]

I run my thumb over the edge. He’s sanded it smooth.

[rInside:9]

**Rowan:** Kuha ako ng tubig.

[rInside:10]

He heads toward the kitchen.

[rInside:11]

I set my bag beside the chair and open the inside pocket.

[rInside:12]

My cards are still there.

[rInside:13]

So is the envelope I brought with me.

[rInside:14]

Yellow, though not as bright as it used to be.

[rInside:15]

I hadn’t planned to take it out yet.

[rInside:16]

The last time I held it like this, I was standing in my room in Manila.

## A Saint Luis morning [d2Morning]

[Background: d2-bedroom-morning; time: day; day: 2.]

[Music: back-together.]

[Rowan sprite: hidden; CGs take precedence while visible.]

[d2Morning:0]

A rooster announces the morning like nobody asked him to keep it down.

[d2Morning:1]

Another one joins in.

[d2Morning:2]

**MC:** Oo na. Gising na.

[d2Morning:3]

I stretch beneath the blanket. The Saint Luis heat has already found me.

[d2Morning:4]

In Manila, I’d be complaining by now. Here, I stay still for another moment.

[d2Morning:5]

A breeze slips through the window. Leaves rustle outside.

[d2Morning:6]

Somewhere beyond the fence, two neighbors are discussing someone else’s business at a perfectly unreasonable volume.

[d2Morning:7]

**MC:** May morning briefing na ang mga marites.

[d2Morning:8]

I smile into my pillow. Some things never change.

[d2Morning:9]

Eventually, my stomach convinces me to sit up. Maybe I can make breakfast.

[d2Morning:10]

I reach for my phone to check the time. My smile disappears.

[d2Morning:11]

Notifications fill the screen. Mom. Dad. Again. And again. Even on SG.

[d2Morning:12]

**MC:** Paano nila nahanap ’to?

[d2Morning:13]

I stare at the account names. One person comes to mind almost immediately.

[d2Morning:14]

*Sevi. He knows this account.*

[d2Morning:15]

That doesn’t prove anything. Still, my jaw tightens.

## Too many notifications [d2PhoneOpen]

[Background: d2-bedroom-morning; time: day; day: 2.]

[Music: lost-contact.]

[Rowan sprite: hidden; CGs take precedence while visible.]

[d2PhoneOpen:0]

*Maybe one of them just wants to know if I arrived safely.*

[d2PhoneOpen:1]

I hate how quickly I reach for that possibility. But I open the notifications anyway.

[d2PhoneOpen:2]

**Phone:** Dad — 60 missed calls. Mom — 34 missed calls.

[d2PhoneOpen:3]

I check the numbers twice. As if they might change.

[d2PhoneOpen:4]

Then I open Mom’s messages.

[d2PhoneOpen:5]

**Mom · SG:** Nasaan ka na?

[d2PhoneOpen:6]

**Mom · SG:** Sagutin mo ang tawag namin.

[d2PhoneOpen:7]

**Mom · SG:** Akala mo hindi namin alam kung saan ka pupunta?

[d2PhoneOpen:8]

**Mom · SG:** You are in big trouble.

[d2PhoneOpen:9]

**Mom · SG:** Bakit hindi ka na lang makinig sa amin?

[d2PhoneOpen:10]

**Mom · SG:** How could you do something so stupid?

[d2PhoneOpen:11]

There are more. I scroll. Then stop.

[d2PhoneOpen:12]

Dad’s messages aren’t much different.

[d2PhoneOpen:13]

**Dad · SG:** Answer your mother.

[d2PhoneOpen:14]

**Dad · SG:** Umuwi ka. Pag-uusapan natin ito.

[d2PhoneOpen:15]

**Dad · SG:** Hindi puwedeng basta ka na lang umaalis kapag ayaw mo ang sinasabi namin.

[d2PhoneOpen:16]

I press my lips together. So much for hoping.

[d2PhoneOpen:17]

A different name appears below theirs. Sevrine Buenaventura.

[d2PhoneOpen:18]

*Four messages. Of course.*

[d2PhoneOpen:19]

**Sevi · SG:** Hey, Dos. Where are you?

[d2PhoneOpen:20]

**Sevi · SG:** Your parents keep asking if you told me anything.

[d2PhoneOpen:21]

**Sevi · SG:** Ayoko namang makialam sa problema ninyo.

[d2PhoneOpen:22]

**Sevi · SG:** Kausapin mo na lang sila, okay? Bye, Dos.

[d2PhoneOpen:23]

I read the third message again. Ayaw niyang makialam. Convenient.

[d2PhoneOpen:24]

**MC:** Hanggang dito ba naman.

[d2PhoneOpen:25]

Nothing in his messages tells me whether he gave them my account.

[d2PhoneOpen:26]

*But he’s talking as if nothing happened between us. As if he wasn’t part of the reason I finally left.*

[d2PhoneOpen:27]

My fingers tighten around the phone. For a second, I consider replying.

[d2PhoneOpen:28]

Then I close the conversation. Not this morning.

[d2PhoneOpen:29]

I silence the notifications and put the phone down.

**Next:** d2GetUp.

[aTopic0_lola_yes:5]

**Rowan:** No.

[Rowan sprite: adult; CGs take precedence while visible.]

## Dos [aTopic2_school_rival]

[Background: reunion-porch; time: day; day: 2.]

[Music: catching-up.]

[Rowan sprite: adult; CGs take precedence while visible.]

[aTopic2_school_rival:0]

**MC:** He’s my so-called academic rival.

[aTopic2_school_rival:1 · Expression: neutral]

**Rowan:** So-called?

[aTopic2_school_rival:2]

**MC:** Classmates namin ang nag-decide. Ngayon lahat na lang kino-compare.

[aTopic2_school_rival:3]

**MC:** “Dos” ’yung tawag niya sa ’kin.

[aTopic2_school_rival:4]

**Rowan:** As in second?

[aTopic2_school_rival:5]

**MC:** Yeah.

[aTopic2_school_rival:6 · Expression: concerned]

Rowan’s smile fades.

[aTopic2_school_rival:7]

**MC:** Student council president pa siya, so laging may reason para ma-excuse.

[aTopic2_school_rival:8]

**MC:** A meeting. An event. Something important.

[aTopic2_school_rival:9]

**MC:** I know some of it is legitimate. But everyone else has to work around it.

[aTopic2_school_rival:10]

**Rowan:** Tapos ikaw ’yung sumasalo?

[aTopic2_school_rival:11]

**MC:** Minsan. O dapat okay lang sa ’kin.

[aTopic2_school_rival:12]

**MC:** ’Yon ’yung nakakainis. Babalik siya na parang walang nangyari.

[aTopic2_school_rival:13]

**MC:** Calls me Dos. Makes another joke.

[aTopic2_school_rival:14]

I realize I’m gripping my glass. I put it down.

[aTopic2_school_rival:15]

**MC:** Isa siya sa dahilan kung bakit hindi na ako sumasali sa orgs.

[aTopic2_school_rival:16]

**MC:** And part of why I—

[aTopic2_school_rival:17]

I stop. Rowan waits.

[aTopic2_school_rival:18]

**MC:** Sorry. Ang dami ko nang nasabi.

[aTopic2_school_rival:19]

**Rowan:** Hindi mo kailangang mag-sorry sa pagkukuwento.

[aTopic2_school_rival:20]

**MC:** Sa ibang araw na lang ’yung iba?

[aTopic2_school_rival:21]

**Rowan:** Of course.

[aTopic2_school_rival:22]

He leans back.

[aTopic2_school_rival:23]

**Rowan:** Gusto mo bang may sabihin ako, o dito muna tayo?

[aTopic2_school_rival:24]

**MC:** Dito muna. For now.

[aTopic2_school_rival:25]

**Rowan:** Okay.

**Next:** aTopics6.

## What comes next [aTopic2_rowan_start]

[Background: reunion-porch; time: day; day: 2.]

[Music: catching-up.]

[Rowan sprite: adult; CGs take precedence while visible.]

[aTopic2_rowan_start:0]

**MC:** Ikaw naman?

[aTopic2_rowan_start:1 · Expression: neutral]

**Rowan:** Me?

[aTopic2_rowan_start:2]

**MC:** Yeah. Kumusta ka?

[aTopic2_rowan_start:3 · Expression: smile]

**Rowan:** Okay naman ako.

[aTopic2_rowan_start:4]

He looks toward the gate. Then back at me.

[aTopic2_rowan_start:5 · Expression: embarrassed]

**Rowan:** Medyo automatic ’yung sagot ko.

[aTopic2_rowan_start:6]

**MC:** Hindi naman kailangang ’yung madaling sagot.

[aTopic2_rowan_start:7]

He rubs the back of his neck.

[aTopic2_rowan_start:8]

**Rowan:** Ang dami kong gustong ikuwento. Hindi ko lang alam saan magsisimula.

[aTopic2_rowan_start:9]

**MC:** Hindi naman kailangang matapos ngayon.

[aTopic2_rowan_start:10 · Expression: smile]

He notices me repeating his words. His smile comes back.

[aTopic2_rowan_start:11]

**Rowan:** Right.

[aTopic2_rowan_start:12]

**Rowan:** For now… masaya akong nandito ka.

[aTopic2_rowan_start:13]

**MC:** I’m glad too.

[aTopic2_rowan_start:14]

**Rowan:** Inaasikaso ko ’yung bahay. Tinutulungan si Mama. Nagtatrabaho kapag may pagkakataon.

[aTopic2_rowan_start:15]

He pauses.

[aTopic2_rowan_start:16 · Expression: neutral]

**Rowan:** And thinking about what comes next.

[aTopic2_rowan_start:17]

**MC:** Anong gusto mong gawin?

[aTopic2_rowan_start:18]

**Rowan:** I’m still figuring out how to say it without talking myself out of it.

[aTopic2_rowan_start:19]

**MC:** Sabihin mo na lang kapag ready ka na.

[aTopic2_rowan_start:20 · Expression: smile]

**Rowan:** Thanks.

[aTopic2_rowan_start:21]

His knee bumps mine when he shifts. “Sorry,” he says, adjusting his chair.

[aTopic2_rowan_start:22]

**Rowan:** One thing hasn’t changed.

[aTopic2_rowan_start:23]

**MC:** What?

[aTopic2_rowan_start:24]

**Rowan:** Gusto pa rin kitang kasama.

[aTopic2_rowan_start:25 · Only if gardenGreeting = "whatcha"]

**MC:** Kahit ang dami kong reklamo?

[aTopic2_rowan_start:26 · Only if gardenGreeting = "hey"]

**MC:** Kahit ang dami kong reklamo?

[aTopic2_rowan_start:27 · Only if gardenGreeting = "poke"]

**MC:** Kahit ang dami kong reklamo?

[aTopic2_rowan_start:28 · Only if gardenGreeting = "scare"]

**MC:** Kahit ginugulat kita sa garden?

[aTopic2_rowan_start:29 · Expression: playful]

**Rowan:** May mga pinapalampas naman ako.

[aTopic2_rowan_start:30]

I laugh. He looks pleased with himself.

**Next:** aTopics10.

**MC:** He’s my so-called academic rival.

[aTopic3_school_rival:1 · Expression: neutral]

**Rowan:** So-called?

[aTopic3_school_rival:2]

**MC:** Classmates namin ang nag-decide. Ngayon lahat na lang kino-compare.

[aTopic3_school_rival:3]

**MC:** “Dos” ’yung tawag niya sa ’kin.

[aTopic3_school_rival:4]

**Rowan:** As in second?

[aTopic3_school_rival:5]

**MC:** Yeah.

[aTopic3_school_rival:6 · Expression: concerned]

Rowan’s smile fades.

[aTopic3_school_rival:7]

**MC:** Student council president pa siya, so laging may reason para ma-excuse.

[aTopic3_school_rival:8]

**MC:** A meeting. An event. Something important.

[aTopic3_school_rival:9]

**MC:** I know some of it is legitimate. But everyone else has to work around it.

[aTopic3_school_rival:10]

**Rowan:** Tapos ikaw ’yung sumasalo?

[aTopic3_school_rival:11]

**MC:** Minsan. O dapat okay lang sa ’kin.

[aTopic3_school_rival:12]

**MC:** ’Yon ’yung nakakainis. Babalik siya na parang walang nangyari.

[aTopic3_school_rival:13]

**MC:** Calls me Dos. Makes another joke.

[aTopic3_school_rival:14]

I realize I’m gripping my glass. I put it down.

[aTopic3_school_rival:15]

**MC:** Isa siya sa dahilan kung bakit hindi na ako sumasali sa orgs.

[aTopic3_school_rival:16]

**MC:** And part of why I—

[aTopic3_school_rival:17]

I stop. Rowan waits.

[aTopic3_school_rival:18]

**MC:** Sorry. Ang dami ko nang nasabi.

[aTopic3_school_rival:19]

**Rowan:** Hindi mo kailangang mag-sorry sa pagkukuwento.

[aTopic3_school_rival:20]

**MC:** Sa ibang araw na lang ’yung iba?

[aTopic3_school_rival:21]

**Rowan:** Of course.

[aTopic3_school_rival:22]

He leans back.

[aTopic3_school_rival:23]

**Rowan:** Gusto mo bang may sabihin ako, o dito muna tayo?

[aTopic3_school_rival:24]

**MC:** Dito muna. For now.

[aTopic3_school_rival:25]

**Rowan:** Okay.

**Next:** aTopics7.

## What comes next [aTopic3_rowan_start]

[Background: reunion-porch; time: day; day: 2.]

[Music: catching-up.]

[Rowan sprite: adult; CGs take precedence while visible.]

[aTopic3_rowan_start:0]

**MC:** Ikaw naman?

[aTopic3_rowan_start:1 · Expression: neutral]

**Rowan:** Me?

[aTopic3_rowan_start:2]

**MC:** Yeah. Kumusta ka?

[aTopic3_rowan_start:3 · Expression: smile]

**Rowan:** Okay naman ako.

[aTopic3_rowan_start:4]

He looks toward the gate. Then back at me.

[aTopic3_rowan_start:5 · Expression: embarrassed]

**Rowan:** Medyo automatic ’yung sagot ko.

[aTopic3_rowan_start:6]

**MC:** Hindi naman kailangang ’yung madaling sagot.

[aTopic3_rowan_start:7]

He rubs the back of his neck.

[aTopic3_rowan_start:8]

**Rowan:** Ang dami kong gustong ikuwento. Hindi ko lang alam saan magsisimula.

[aTopic3_rowan_start:9]

**MC:** Hindi naman kailangang matapos ngayon.

[aTopic3_rowan_start:10 · Expression: smile]

He notices me repeating his words. His smile comes back.

[aTopic3_rowan_start:11]

**Rowan:** Right.

[aTopic3_rowan_start:12]

**Rowan:** For now… masaya akong nandito ka.

[aTopic3_rowan_start:13]

**MC:** I’m glad too.

[aTopic3_rowan_start:14]

**Rowan:** Inaasikaso ko ’yung bahay. Tinutulungan si Mama. Nagtatrabaho kapag may pagkakataon.

[aTopic3_rowan_start:15]

He pauses.

[aTopic3_rowan_start:16 · Expression: neutral]

**Rowan:** And thinking about what comes next.

[aTopic3_rowan_start:17]

**MC:** Anong gusto mong gawin?

[aTopic3_rowan_start:18]

**Rowan:** I’m still figuring out how to say it without talking myself out of it.

[aTopic3_rowan_start:19]

**MC:** Sabihin mo na lang kapag ready ka na.

[aTopic3_rowan_start:20 · Expression: smile]

**Rowan:** Thanks.

[aTopic3_rowan_start:21]

His knee bumps mine when he shifts. “Sorry,” he says, adjusting his chair.

[aTopic3_rowan_start:22]

**Rowan:** One thing hasn’t changed.

[aTopic3_rowan_start:23]

**MC:** What?

[aTopic3_rowan_start:24]

**Rowan:** Gusto pa rin kitang kasama.

[aTopic3_rowan_start:25 · Only if gardenGreeting = "whatcha"]

**MC:** Kahit ang dami kong reklamo?

[aTopic3_rowan_start:26 · Only if gardenGreeting = "hey"]

[aTopic3_rowan_start:27 · Only if gardenGreeting = "poke"]

**MC:** Kahit ang dami kong reklamo?

[aTopic3_rowan_start:28 · Only if gardenGreeting = "scare"]

**MC:** Kahit ginugulat kita sa garden?

[aTopic3_rowan_start:29 · Expression: playful]

**Rowan:** May mga pinapalampas naman ako.

[aTopic3_rowan_start:30]

I laugh. He looks pleased with himself.

## What happened [aTopic4_lola_yes]

[Background: reunion-porch; time: day; day: 2.]

[Music: letters.]

[Rowan sprite: adult; CGs take precedence while visible.]

[aTopic4_lola_yes:0]

**MC:** Yes. Please.

[aTopic4_lola_yes:1 · Expression: sad]

Rowan looks down at his hands.

[aTopic4_lola_yes:2]

**Rowan:** Mas mabilis na siyang mapagod. Minsan kailangan niyang huminto at umupo kahit hindi pa tapos ’yung ginagawa niya.

[aTopic4_lola_yes:3]

**Rowan:** Nag-aalala ’yung doctor sa puso niya. Gusto niyang magpatingin si Lola sa specialist sa city.

[aTopic4_lola_yes:4]

**MC:** Pumunta siya?

[aTopic4_lola_yes:5]

**Rowan:** No.

[aTopic4_lola_yes:6]

I wait for him to continue.

[aTopic4_lola_yes:7]

**Rowan:** Inaalala niya ’yung gastos. Saka ’yung bahay. Tuwing binabanggit namin, may ibang dahilan para ipagpaliban.

[aTopic4_lola_yes:8]

**Rowan:** Sasamahan sana siya ni Mama. Ako rin.

[aTopic4_lola_yes:9]

**MC:** Paano ’yung parents ko?

[aTopic4_lola_yes:10 · Expression: concerned]

**Rowan:** Nagpatulong siya sa kanila para maayos ’yung biyahe.

[aTopic4_lola_yes:11]

**MC:** You heard her?

[aTopic4_lola_yes:12]

**Rowan:** Nando’n ako noong tumawag siya. Pero side niya lang ’yung narinig ko.

[aTopic4_lola_yes:13]

He runs his thumb along the seam of his trousers.

[aTopic4_lola_yes:14]

**Rowan:** After no’n, ayaw na raw niyang magtanong ulit.

[aTopic4_lola_yes:15]

**MC:** Anong sinabi nila sa kanya?

[aTopic4_lola_yes:16]

**Rowan:** I don’t know.

[aTopic4_lola_yes:17]

**MC:** Sabi nila okay lang siya.

[aTopic4_lola_yes:18]

He looks at me.

[aTopic4_lola_yes:19]

**Rowan:** Hindi mo alam ’yung tungkol sa specialist?

[aTopic4_lola_yes:20]

I shake my head.

[aTopic4_lola_yes:21]

**Rowan:** Akala ko alam mong may sakit siya. Hindi mo lang alam… kung gaano na kalala.

[aTopic4_lola_yes:22]

I can’t think of anything to say.

[aTopic4_lola_yes:23]

**MC:** Wala akong alam sa lahat ng ’to.

[aTopic4_lola_yes:24 · Expression: sad]

**Rowan:** I’m sorry.

[aTopic4_lola_yes:25]

He shifts, then stops.

[aTopic4_lola_yes:26]

**Rowan:** Itutuloy ko ba?

## Tita Mayumi [aTopic5_mayumi_start]

[Background: reunion-porch; time: day; day: 2.]

[Music: catching-up.]

[Rowan sprite: adult; CGs take precedence while visible.]

[aTopic5_mayumi_start:0]

**MC:** Kumusta si Tita Mayumi?

[aTopic5_mayumi_start:1 · Expression: neutral]

**Rowan:** Busy. Ayaw pa ring aminin.

[aTopic5_mayumi_start:2]

**MC:** That sounds familiar.

[aTopic5_mayumi_start:3 · Expression: smile]

**Rowan:** Matutuwa siyang bumalik ka.

[aTopic5_mayumi_start:4]

**MC:** Gusto ko siyang makita.

[aTopic5_mayumi_start:5]

**Rowan:** Sasabihin ko. Tapos kailangan mong tanggapin lahat ng pagkaing sa tingin niya kulang sa diet mo.

[aTopic5_mayumi_start:6]

**MC:** I can live with that.

[aTopic5_mayumi_start:7]

He smiles.

[aTopic5_mayumi_start:8]

**MC:** Saan siya nagtatrabaho ngayon?

[aTopic5_mayumi_start:9]

**Rowan:** Customer service in the next town. Calls, complaints, helping people with their accounts.

[aTopic5_mayumi_start:10]

**Rowan:** Tapos pag-uwi niya, may energy pa rin siyang mangumusta.

[aTopic5_mayumi_start:11]

**MC:** Tinutulungan mo siya?

[aTopic5_mayumi_start:12]

**Rowan:** Sinusubukan ko. Kadalasan pinapatapos niya muna ’yung ginagawa ko rito.

[aTopic5_mayumi_start:13]

He looks down at his hands.

[aTopic5_mayumi_start:14 · Expression: playful]

**Rowan:** Unfair nga. Ang lakas-lakas ko, pero naipasok na niya ’yung groceries bago pa ako makarating sa pinto.

[aTopic5_mayumi_start:15]

**MC:** Baka mas mabilis lang siya sa ’yo.

[aTopic5_mayumi_start:16]

**Rowan:** Oo. Huwag mong sabihin na inamin ko.

**Next:** aTopics7.

## What happened [aTopic6_lola_yes]

[Background: reunion-porch; time: day; day: 2.]

[Music: letters.]

[Rowan sprite: adult; CGs take precedence while visible.]

[aTopic6_lola_yes:0]

**MC:** Yes. Please.

[aTopic6_lola_yes:1 · Expression: sad]

Rowan looks down at his hands.

[aTopic6_lola_yes:2]

**Rowan:** Mas mabilis na siyang mapagod. Minsan kailangan niyang huminto at umupo kahit hindi pa tapos ’yung ginagawa niya.

[aTopic6_lola_yes:3]

**Rowan:** Nag-aalala ’yung doctor sa puso niya. Gusto niyang magpatingin si Lola sa specialist sa city.

[aTopic6_lola_yes:4]

**MC:** Pumunta siya?

[aTopic6_lola_yes:5]

**Rowan:** No.

[aTopic6_lola_yes:6]

I wait for him to continue.

[aTopic6_lola_yes:7]

**Rowan:** Inaalala niya ’yung gastos. Saka ’yung bahay. Tuwing binabanggit namin, may ibang dahilan para ipagpaliban.

[aTopic6_lola_yes:8]

**Rowan:** Sasamahan sana siya ni Mama. Ako rin.

[aTopic6_lola_yes:9]

**MC:** Paano ’yung parents ko?

[aTopic6_lola_yes:10 · Expression: concerned]

**Rowan:** Nagpatulong siya sa kanila para maayos ’yung biyahe.

[aTopic6_lola_yes:11]

**MC:** You heard her?

[aTopic6_lola_yes:12]

**Rowan:** Nando’n ako noong tumawag siya. Pero side niya lang ’yung narinig ko.

[aTopic6_lola_yes:13]

He runs his thumb along the seam of his trousers.

[aTopic6_lola_yes:14]

**Rowan:** After no’n, ayaw na raw niyang magtanong ulit.

[aTopic6_lola_yes:15]

**MC:** Anong sinabi nila sa kanya?

[aTopic6_lola_yes:16]

**Rowan:** I don’t know.

[aTopic6_lola_yes:17]

**MC:** Sabi nila okay lang siya.

[aTopic6_lola_yes:18]

He looks at me.

[aTopic6_lola_yes:19]

**Rowan:** Hindi mo alam ’yung tungkol sa specialist?

[aTopic6_lola_yes:20]

I shake my head.

[aTopic6_lola_yes:21]

**Rowan:** Akala ko alam mong may sakit siya. Hindi mo lang alam… kung gaano na kalala.

[aTopic6_lola_yes:22]

I can’t think of anything to say.

[aTopic6_lola_yes:23]

**MC:** Wala akong alam sa lahat ng ’to.

[aTopic6_lola_yes:24 · Expression: sad]

**Rowan:** I’m sorry.

[aTopic6_lola_yes:25]

He shifts, then stops.

[aTopic6_lola_yes:26]

**Rowan:** Itutuloy ko ba?

### Player choices

## What happened [aTopic8_lola_yes]

[Background: reunion-porch; time: day; day: 2.]

[Music: letters.]

[Rowan sprite: adult; CGs take precedence while visible.]

[aTopic8_lola_yes:0]

**MC:** Yes. Please.

[aTopic8_lola_yes:1 · Expression: sad]

Rowan looks down at his hands.

[aTopic8_lola_yes:2]

**Rowan:** Mas mabilis na siyang mapagod. Minsan kailangan niyang huminto at umupo kahit hindi pa tapos ’yung ginagawa niya.

[aTopic8_lola_yes:3]

**Rowan:** Nag-aalala ’yung doctor sa puso niya. Gusto niyang magpatingin si Lola sa specialist sa city.

[aTopic8_lola_yes:4]

**MC:** Pumunta siya?

[aTopic8_lola_yes:5]

**Rowan:** No.

[aTopic8_lola_yes:6]

I wait for him to continue.

[aTopic8_lola_yes:7]

**Rowan:** Inaalala niya ’yung gastos. Saka ’yung bahay. Tuwing binabanggit namin, may ibang dahilan para ipagpaliban.

[aTopic8_lola_yes:8]

**Rowan:** Sasamahan sana siya ni Mama. Ako rin.

[aTopic8_lola_yes:9]

**MC:** Paano ’yung parents ko?

[aTopic8_lola_yes:10 · Expression: concerned]

**Rowan:** Nagpatulong siya sa kanila para maayos ’yung biyahe.

[aTopic8_lola_yes:11]

**MC:** You heard her?

[aTopic8_lola_yes:12]

**Rowan:** Nando’n ako noong tumawag siya. Pero side niya lang ’yung narinig ko.

[aTopic8_lola_yes:13]

He runs his thumb along the seam of his trousers.

[aTopic8_lola_yes:14]

**Rowan:** After no’n, ayaw na raw niyang magtanong ulit.

[aTopic8_lola_yes:15]

**MC:** Anong sinabi nila sa kanya?

[aTopic8_lola_yes:16]

**Rowan:** I don’t know.

[aTopic8_lola_yes:17]

**MC:** Sabi nila okay lang siya.

[aTopic8_lola_yes:18]

He looks at me.

[aTopic8_lola_yes:19]

**Rowan:** Hindi mo alam ’yung tungkol sa specialist?

[aTopic8_lola_yes:20]

I shake my head.

[aTopic8_lola_yes:21]

**Rowan:** Akala ko alam mong may sakit siya. Hindi mo lang alam… kung gaano na kalala.

[aTopic8_lola_yes:22]

I can’t think of anything to say.

[aTopic8_lola_yes:23]

**MC:** Wala akong alam sa lahat ng ’to.

[aTopic8_lola_yes:24 · Expression: sad]

**Rowan:** I’m sorry.

[aTopic8_lola_yes:25]

[aTopic8_lola_yes:26]

## Dos [aTopic8_school_rival]

[Background: reunion-porch; time: day; day: 2.]

[Music: catching-up.]

[Rowan sprite: adult; CGs take precedence while visible.]

[aTopic8_school_rival:0]

**MC:** He’s my so-called academic rival.

[aTopic8_school_rival:1 · Expression: neutral]

**Rowan:** So-called?

[aTopic8_school_rival:2]

**MC:** Classmates namin ang nag-decide. Ngayon lahat na lang kino-compare.

[aTopic8_school_rival:3]

**MC:** “Dos” ’yung tawag niya sa ’kin.

[aTopic8_school_rival:4]

**Rowan:** As in second?

[aTopic8_school_rival:5]

**MC:** Yeah.

[aTopic8_school_rival:6 · Expression: concerned]

Rowan’s smile fades.

[aTopic8_school_rival:7]

**MC:** Student council president pa siya, so laging may reason para ma-excuse.

[aTopic8_school_rival:8]

**MC:** A meeting. An event. Something important.

[aTopic8_school_rival:9]

**MC:** I know some of it is legitimate. But everyone else has to work around it.

[aTopic8_school_rival:10]

**Rowan:** Tapos ikaw ’yung sumasalo?

[aTopic8_school_rival:11]

**MC:** Minsan. O dapat okay lang sa ’kin.

[aTopic8_school_rival:12]

**MC:** ’Yon ’yung nakakainis. Babalik siya na parang walang nangyari.

[aTopic8_school_rival:13]

**MC:** Calls me Dos. Makes another joke.

[aTopic8_school_rival:14]

I realize I’m gripping my glass. I put it down.

[aTopic8_school_rival:15]

**MC:** Isa siya sa dahilan kung bakit hindi na ako sumasali sa orgs.

[aTopic8_school_rival:16]

**MC:** And part of why I—

[aTopic8_school_rival:17]

I stop. Rowan waits.

[aTopic8_school_rival:18]

**MC:** Sorry. Ang dami ko nang nasabi.

[aTopic8_school_rival:19]

**Rowan:** Hindi mo kailangang mag-sorry sa pagkukuwento.

[aTopic8_school_rival:20]

**MC:** Sa ibang araw na lang ’yung iba?

[aTopic8_school_rival:21]

**Rowan:** Of course.

[aTopic8_school_rival:22]

He leans back.

[aTopic8_school_rival:23]

**Rowan:** Gusto mo bang may sabihin ako, o dito muna tayo?

[aTopic8_school_rival:24]

**MC:** Dito muna. For now.

[aTopic8_school_rival:25]

**Rowan:** Okay.

[Background: reunion-porch; time: day; day: 2.]

[Music: catching-up.]

[Rowan sprite: adult; CGs take precedence while visible.]

[aTopic9_mayumi_start:0]

**MC:** Kumusta si Tita Mayumi?

[aTopic9_mayumi_start:1 · Expression: neutral]

**Rowan:** Busy. Ayaw pa ring aminin.

[aTopic9_mayumi_start:2]

**MC:** That sounds familiar.

[aTopic9_mayumi_start:3 · Expression: smile]

**Rowan:** Matutuwa siyang bumalik ka.

[aTopic9_mayumi_start:4]

**MC:** Gusto ko siyang makita.

[aTopic9_mayumi_start:5]

**Rowan:** Sasabihin ko. Tapos kailangan mong tanggapin lahat ng pagkaing sa tingin niya kulang sa diet mo.

[aTopic9_mayumi_start:6]

**MC:** I can live with that.

[aTopic9_mayumi_start:7]

He smiles.

[aTopic9_mayumi_start:8]

**MC:** Saan siya nagtatrabaho ngayon?

[aTopic9_mayumi_start:9]

**Rowan:** Customer service in the next town. Calls, complaints, helping people with their accounts.

[aTopic9_mayumi_start:10]

**Rowan:** Tapos pag-uwi niya, may energy pa rin siyang mangumusta.

[aTopic9_mayumi_start:11]

**MC:** Tinutulungan mo siya?

[aTopic9_mayumi_start:12]

**Rowan:** Sinusubukan ko. Kadalasan pinapatapos niya muna ’yung ginagawa ko rito.

[aTopic9_mayumi_start:13]

He looks down at his hands.

[aTopic9_mayumi_start:14 · Expression: playful]

**Rowan:** Unfair nga. Ang lakas-lakas ko, pero naipasok na niya ’yung groceries bago pa ako makarating sa pinto.

[aTopic9_mayumi_start:15]

**MC:** Baka mas mabilis lang siya sa ’yo.

[aTopic9_mayumi_start:16]

**Rowan:** Oo. Huwag mong sabihin na inamin ko.

**Next:** aTopics11.

## What happened [aTopic10_lola_yes]

[Background: reunion-porch; time: day; day: 2.]

[Music: letters.]

[Rowan sprite: adult; CGs take precedence while visible.]

[aTopic10_lola_yes:0]

**MC:** Yes. Please.

[aTopic10_lola_yes:1 · Expression: sad]

Rowan looks down at his hands.

[aTopic10_lola_yes:2]

**Rowan:** Mas mabilis na siyang mapagod. Minsan kailangan niyang huminto at umupo kahit hindi pa tapos ’yung ginagawa niya.

[aTopic10_lola_yes:3]

**Rowan:** Nag-aalala ’yung doctor sa puso niya. Gusto niyang magpatingin si Lola sa specialist sa city.

[aTopic10_lola_yes:4]

**MC:** Pumunta siya?

[aTopic10_lola_yes:5]

**Rowan:** No.

[aTopic10_lola_yes:6]

I wait for him to continue.

[aTopic10_lola_yes:7]

**Rowan:** Inaalala niya ’yung gastos. Saka ’yung bahay. Tuwing binabanggit namin, may ibang dahilan para ipagpaliban.

[aTopic10_lola_yes:8]

**Rowan:** Sasamahan sana siya ni Mama. Ako rin.

[aTopic10_lola_yes:9]

**MC:** Paano ’yung parents ko?

[aTopic10_lola_yes:10 · Expression: concerned]

**Rowan:** Nagpatulong siya sa kanila para maayos ’yung biyahe.

[aTopic10_lola_yes:11]

**MC:** You heard her?

[aTopic10_lola_yes:12]

**Rowan:** Nando’n ako noong tumawag siya. Pero side niya lang ’yung narinig ko.

[aTopic10_lola_yes:13]

He runs his thumb along the seam of his trousers.

[aTopic10_lola_yes:14]

**Rowan:** After no’n, ayaw na raw niyang magtanong ulit.

[aTopic10_lola_yes:15]

**MC:** Anong sinabi nila sa kanya?

[aTopic10_lola_yes:16]

**Rowan:** I don’t know.

[aTopic10_lola_yes:17]

**MC:** Sabi nila okay lang siya.

[aTopic10_lola_yes:18]

He looks at me.

[aTopic10_lola_yes:19]

**Rowan:** Hindi mo alam ’yung tungkol sa specialist?

[aTopic10_lola_yes:20]

I shake my head.

[aTopic10_lola_yes:21]

**Rowan:** Akala ko alam mong may sakit siya. Hindi mo lang alam… kung gaano na kalala.

[aTopic10_lola_yes:22]

I can’t think of anything to say.

[aTopic10_lola_yes:23]

**MC:** Wala akong alam sa lahat ng ’to.

[aTopic10_lola_yes:24 · Expression: sad]

**Rowan:** I’m sorry.

[aTopic10_lola_yes:25]

He shifts, then stops.

[aTopic10_lola_yes:26]

**Rowan:** Itutuloy ko ba?

## The rest of it [aTopic10_lola_details]

*The letter.*

[aTopic10_lola_details:3]

*She was still telling him what to write.*

[aTopic10_lola_details:4 · Expression: sad]

**Rowan:** May mga araw na mas okay siya. Akala namin, may oras pa.

[aTopic10_lola_details:5]

**Rowan:** Tapos after a few months, lumala. Dinala namin siya sa ospital dito.

[aTopic10_lola_details:6]

He rubs his thumb against his palm.

[aTopic10_lola_details:7]

**Rowan:** Doon siya namatay.

[aTopic10_lola_details:8]

**MC:** At walang nagsabi sa ’kin kung gaano siya kasakit.

[aTopic10_lola_details:9]

**Rowan:** Dapat sinabi sa ’yo.

[Background: reunion-porch; time: day; day: 2.]

[Music: letters.]

[Rowan sprite: adult; CGs take precedence while visible.]

[aTopic10_lola_angry:0]

[aTopic10_lola_angry:3]

[aTopic10_lola_angry:5]

**Rowan:** Nagalit din ako.

## Beside me [aTopic10_lola_sad]

[Music: letters.]

[Rowan sprite: adult; CGs take precedence while visible.]

[aTopic10_lola_sad:0]

## Too much at once [aTopic10_lola_numb]

[Background: reunion-porch; time: day; day: 2.]

[Music: letters.]

[Rowan sprite: adult; CGs take precedence while visible.]

[aTopic10_lola_numb:0]

I stare at a crack in the porch floor.

[aTopic10_lola_numb:1]

*I heard everything he said. I just don’t know what to do with it yet.*

[aTopic10_lola_numb:2]

**MC:** Huli na ako.

## Dos [aTopic10_school_rival]

[Background: reunion-porch; time: day; day: 2.]

[Music: catching-up.]

[Rowan sprite: adult; CGs take precedence while visible.]

[aTopic10_school_rival:0]

**MC:** He’s my so-called academic rival.

[aTopic10_school_rival:1 · Expression: neutral]

**Rowan:** So-called?

[aTopic10_school_rival:2]

**MC:** Classmates namin ang nag-decide. Ngayon lahat na lang kino-compare.

[aTopic10_school_rival:3]

**MC:** “Dos” ’yung tawag niya sa ’kin.

[aTopic10_school_rival:4]

**Rowan:** As in second?

[aTopic10_school_rival:5]

**MC:** Yeah.

[aTopic10_school_rival:6 · Expression: concerned]

Rowan’s smile fades.

[aTopic10_school_rival:7]

**MC:** Student council president pa siya, so laging may reason para ma-excuse.

[aTopic10_school_rival:8]

**MC:** A meeting. An event. Something important.

[aTopic10_school_rival:9]

**MC:** I know some of it is legitimate. But everyone else has to work around it.

[aTopic10_school_rival:10]

**Rowan:** Tapos ikaw ’yung sumasalo?

[aTopic10_school_rival:11]

**MC:** Minsan. O dapat okay lang sa ’kin.

[aTopic10_school_rival:12]

**MC:** ’Yon ’yung nakakainis. Babalik siya na parang walang nangyari.

[aTopic10_school_rival:13]

**MC:** Calls me Dos. Makes another joke.

[aTopic10_school_rival:14]

I realize I’m gripping my glass. I put it down.

[aTopic10_school_rival:15]

**MC:** Isa siya sa dahilan kung bakit hindi na ako sumasali sa orgs.

[aTopic10_school_rival:16]

**MC:** And part of why I—

[aTopic10_school_rival:17]

I stop. Rowan waits.

[aTopic10_school_rival:18]

**MC:** Sorry. Ang dami ko nang nasabi.

[aTopic10_school_rival:19]

**Rowan:** Hindi mo kailangang mag-sorry sa pagkukuwento.

[aTopic10_school_rival:20]

**MC:** Sa ibang araw na lang ’yung iba?

[aTopic10_school_rival:21]

**Rowan:** Of course.

[aTopic10_school_rival:22]

He leans back.

[aTopic10_school_rival:23]

**Rowan:** Gusto mo bang may sabihin ako, o dito muna tayo?

[aTopic10_school_rival:24]

**MC:** Dito muna. For now.

[aTopic10_school_rival:25]

**Rowan:** Okay.

**Next:** aTopics14.

## Ask about Lola [aTopic12_lola_start]

[Background: reunion-porch; time: day; day: 2.]

[Music: letters.]

[Rowan sprite: adult; CGs take precedence while visible.]

[aTopic12_lola_start:0]

**MC:** Ro?

[aTopic12_lola_start:1 · Expression: neutral]

**Rowan:** Hmm?

[aTopic12_lola_start:2]

**MC:** Puwede akong magtanong tungkol kay Lola?

[aTopic12_lola_start:3]

He turns toward me.

[aTopic12_lola_start:4]

**MC:** Sinabi lang nila na namatay siya. Halos wala nang ibang kuwento.

[aTopic12_lola_start:5]

**MC:** Anong nangyari?

[aTopic12_lola_start:6 · Expression: concerned]

**Rowan:** Sasabihin ko ’yung alam ko.

[aTopic12_lola_start:7]

He pauses.

[aTopic12_lola_start:8]

**Rowan:** Gusto mo bang pag-usapan ngayon?

### Player choices

**Choice 1 [aTopic12_lola_start:choice:0]**

Yes. I want to know.

[Continue at: aTopic12_lola_yes. Saved choice / relationship flags: {"porchLola":"heard"}.]

**Choice 2 [aTopic12_lola_start:choice:1]**

Not yet. I thought I was ready.

[Continue at: aTopic12_lola_notYet. Saved choice / relationship flags: {"porchLola":"not-yet"}.]

## Another time [aTopic12_lola_notYet]

[Background: reunion-porch; time: day; day: 2.]

[Music: letters.]

[Rowan sprite: adult; CGs take precedence while visible.]

[aTopic12_lola_notYet:0]

**MC:** Hindi pa pala. Sorry. Akala ko ready na ako.

[aTopic12_lola_notYet:1 · Expression: concerned]

**Rowan:** Hindi mo kailangang mag-sorry.

[aTopic12_lola_notYet:2]

He settles back in his chair.

[aTopic12_lola_notYet:3]

**Rowan:** Another time, then.

## What happened [aTopic12_lola_yes]

[Background: reunion-porch; time: day; day: 2.]

[Music: letters.]

[Rowan sprite: adult; CGs take precedence while visible.]

[aTopic12_lola_yes:0]

**MC:** Yes. Please.

[aTopic12_lola_yes:1 · Expression: sad]

Rowan looks down at his hands.

[aTopic12_lola_yes:2]

**Rowan:** Mas mabilis na siyang mapagod. Minsan kailangan niyang huminto at umupo kahit hindi pa tapos ’yung ginagawa niya.

[aTopic12_lola_yes:3]

**Rowan:** Nag-aalala ’yung doctor sa puso niya. Gusto niyang magpatingin si Lola sa specialist sa city.

[aTopic12_lola_yes:4]

**MC:** Pumunta siya?

[aTopic12_lola_yes:5]

**Rowan:** No.

[aTopic12_lola_yes:6]

I wait for him to continue.

[aTopic12_lola_yes:7]

**Rowan:** Inaalala niya ’yung gastos. Saka ’yung bahay. Tuwing binabanggit namin, may ibang dahilan para ipagpaliban.

[aTopic12_lola_yes:8]

**Rowan:** Sasamahan sana siya ni Mama. Ako rin.

[aTopic12_lola_yes:9]

**MC:** Paano ’yung parents ko?

[aTopic12_lola_yes:10 · Expression: concerned]

**Rowan:** Nagpatulong siya sa kanila para maayos ’yung biyahe.

[aTopic12_lola_yes:11]

**MC:** You heard her?

[aTopic12_lola_yes:12]

**Rowan:** Nando’n ako noong tumawag siya. Pero side niya lang ’yung narinig ko.

[aTopic12_lola_yes:13]

He runs his thumb along the seam of his trousers.

[aTopic12_lola_yes:14]

**Rowan:** After no’n, ayaw na raw niyang magtanong ulit.

[aTopic12_lola_yes:15]

**MC:** Anong sinabi nila sa kanya?

[aTopic12_lola_yes:16]

**Rowan:** I don’t know.

[aTopic12_lola_yes:17]

**MC:** Sabi nila okay lang siya.

[aTopic12_lola_yes:18]

He looks at me.

[aTopic12_lola_yes:19]

**Rowan:** Hindi mo alam ’yung tungkol sa specialist?

[aTopic12_lola_yes:20]

I shake my head.

[aTopic12_lola_yes:21]

**Rowan:** Akala ko alam mong may sakit siya. Hindi mo lang alam… kung gaano na kalala.

[aTopic12_lola_yes:22]

I can’t think of anything to say.

[aTopic12_lola_yes:23]

**MC:** Wala akong alam sa lahat ng ’to.

[aTopic12_lola_yes:24 · Expression: sad]

**Rowan:** I’m sorry.

[aTopic12_lola_yes:25]

He shifts, then stops.

[aTopic12_lola_yes:26]

**Rowan:** Itutuloy ko ba?

### Player choices

**Choice 1 [aTopic12_lola_yes:choice:0]**

“Oo. Sabihin mo.”

[Continue at: aTopic12_lola_details.]

**Choice 2 [aTopic12_lola_yes:choice:1]**

“Sandali lang muna.”

[Continue at: aTopic12_lola_pause.]

**Choice 3 [aTopic12_lola_yes:choice:2]**

“Huwag muna ngayon.”

[Continue at: aTopic12_lola_stop.]

## A minute [aTopic12_lola_pause]

[Background: reunion-porch; time: day; day: 2.]

[Music: letters.]

[Rowan sprite: adult; CGs take precedence while visible.]

[aTopic12_lola_pause:0]

**MC:** Sandali lang muna.

[aTopic12_lola_pause:1 · Expression: concerned]

**Rowan:** Okay.

[aTopic12_lola_pause:2]

A motorbike passes the house. I listen until I can’t hear it anymore.

[aTopic12_lola_pause:3]

Rowan waits beside me.

### Player choices

**Choice 1 [aTopic12_lola_pause:choice:0]**

“Okay. Ituloy mo.”

[Continue at: aTopic12_lola_details.]

## The rest of it [aTopic12_lola_details]

[Background: reunion-porch; time: day; day: 2.]

[Music: letters.]

[Rowan sprite: adult; CGs take precedence while visible.]

[aTopic12_lola_details:0]

**MC:** Kailan nagsimula?

[aTopic12_lola_details:1]

**Rowan:** Ilang araw matapos kong isulat ’yung yellow letter, sumama ’yung pakiramdam niya. Doon sinabi ng doctor na kailangan niyang pumunta.

[aTopic12_lola_details:2]

*The letter.*

[aTopic12_lola_details:3]

*She was still telling him what to write.*

[aTopic12_lola_details:4 · Expression: sad]

**Rowan:** May mga araw na mas okay siya. Akala namin, may oras pa.

[aTopic12_lola_details:5]

**Rowan:** Tapos after a few months, lumala. Dinala namin siya sa ospital dito.

[aTopic12_lola_details:6]

He rubs his thumb against his palm.

[aTopic12_lola_details:7]

**Rowan:** Doon siya namatay.

[aTopic12_lola_details:8]

**MC:** At walang nagsabi sa ’kin kung gaano siya kasakit.

[aTopic12_lola_details:9]

**Rowan:** Dapat sinabi sa ’yo.

## Tita Mayumi [aTopic12_mayumi_start]

[Background: reunion-porch; time: day; day: 2.]

[Music: catching-up.]

[Rowan sprite: adult; CGs take precedence while visible.]

[aTopic12_mayumi_start:0]

**MC:** Kumusta si Tita Mayumi?

[aTopic12_mayumi_start:1 · Expression: neutral]

**Rowan:** Busy. Ayaw pa ring aminin.

[aTopic12_mayumi_start:2]

**MC:** That sounds familiar.

[aTopic12_mayumi_start:3 · Expression: smile]

**Rowan:** Matutuwa siyang bumalik ka.

[aTopic12_mayumi_start:4]

**MC:** Gusto ko siyang makita.

[aTopic12_mayumi_start:5]

**Rowan:** Sasabihin ko. Tapos kailangan mong tanggapin lahat ng pagkaing sa tingin niya kulang sa diet mo.

[aTopic12_mayumi_start:6]

**MC:** I can live with that.

[aTopic12_mayumi_start:7]

He smiles.

[aTopic12_mayumi_start:8]

**MC:** Saan siya nagtatrabaho ngayon?

[aTopic12_mayumi_start:9]

**Rowan:** Customer service in the next town. Calls, complaints, helping people with their accounts.

[aTopic12_mayumi_start:10]

**Rowan:** Tapos pag-uwi niya, may energy pa rin siyang mangumusta.

[aTopic12_mayumi_start:11]

**MC:** Tinutulungan mo siya?

[aTopic12_mayumi_start:12]

**Rowan:** Sinusubukan ko. Kadalasan pinapatapos niya muna ’yung ginagawa ko rito.

[aTopic12_mayumi_start:13]

He looks down at his hands.

[aTopic12_mayumi_start:14 · Expression: playful]

**Rowan:** Unfair nga. Ang lakas-lakas ko, pero naipasok na niya ’yung groceries bago pa ako makarating sa pinto.

[aTopic12_mayumi_start:15]

**MC:** Baka mas mabilis lang siya sa ’yo.

[aTopic12_mayumi_start:16]

**Rowan:** Oo. Huwag mong sabihin na inamin ko.

**Next:** aTopics14.

## The rest of it [aTopic14_lola_details]

[Background: reunion-porch; time: day; day: 2.]

[Music: letters.]

[Rowan sprite: adult; CGs take precedence while visible.]

[aTopic14_lola_details:0]

[aTopic14_lola_details:1]

**Rowan:** Ilang araw matapos kong isulat ’yung yellow letter, sumama ’yung pakiramdam niya. Doon sinabi ng doctor na kailangan niyang pumunta.

[aTopic14_lola_details:2]

*The letter.*

[aTopic14_lola_details:3]

*She was still telling him what to write.*

[aTopic14_lola_details:4 · Expression: sad]

[aTopic14_lola_details:5]

**Rowan:** Tapos after a few months, lumala. Dinala namin siya sa ospital dito.

[aTopic14_lola_details:6]

He rubs his thumb against his palm.

[aTopic14_lola_details:7]

**Rowan:** Doon siya namatay.

[aTopic14_lola_details:8]

**MC:** At walang nagsabi sa ’kin kung gaano siya kasakit.

[aTopic14_lola_details:9]

**Rowan:** Dapat sinabi sa ’yo.

## Appendix — additional story text

### Rowan’s letter

[Rowan’s letter:0]

Hey, {name}.

[Rowan’s letter:1]

Twenty-one na ako last week.

[Rowan’s letter:2]

Nagluto si Lola ng pancit. Para sa ’yo raw ’yung extra serving, pero pinakain din niya sa ’kin bago lumamig.

[Rowan’s letter:3]

Inubos ko. Sorry.

[Rowan’s letter:4]

Hindi ko pa rin sure kung nakakarating ’tong letters. Sabi ng parents mo, busy ka sa school.

[Rowan’s letter:5]

Sana okay naman.

[Rowan’s letter:6]

Konting updates dito:

[Rowan’s letter:7]

Mas matangkad na ako. Talaga. Ako na pinapakuha ni Lola ng gamit sa top shelf, imbes na kumuha siya ng upuan.

[Rowan’s letter:8]

Pero sinasabihan pa rin niya akong mag-ingat. Dangerous work pala ’yung pagkuha ng garapon.

[Rowan’s letter:9]

Mahaba na rin buhok ko.

[Rowan’s letter:10]

Magpapagupit sana ako, pero na-delay nang na-delay. Natatali ko na ngayon.

[Rowan’s letter:11]

Okay lang daw kay Lola basta hindi mapunta sa pagkain.

[Rowan’s letter:12]

Siguradong may masasabi ka rito.

[Rowan’s letter:13]

Tumutulong ako sa bahay. Small repairs lang. Naayos ko ’yung cupboard last week. Two tries lang.

[Rowan’s letter:14]

Siyempre, ikinuwento niya sa Mama mo ’yung first try.

[Rowan’s letter:15]

May mga bagay ditong hindi masyadong nagbago.

[Rowan’s letter:16]

Umaalog pa rin ’yung upuan mo sa mesa.

[Rowan’s letter:17]

Mas mahirap pa ring paakyat ’yung hill.

[Rowan’s letter:18]

Napapatingin pa rin ako kapag may humihinto sa gate.

[Rowan’s letter:19]

Na-miss kita.

[Rowan’s letter:20]

May less awkward sana akong isusulat, pero ’yon talaga ’yung gusto kong sabihin.

[Rowan’s letter:21]

Minsan may nangyayari, tapos naiisip ko, “Kailangan kong ikuwento kay {name}.”

[Rowan’s letter:22]

Tapos maaalala kong wala ka na sa kabilang bahay.

[Rowan’s letter:23]

Hindi mo kailangang i-explain lahat kapag sumulat ka.

[Rowan’s letter:24]

Kuwento mo ’yung lunch mo. O kung may nakakainis na nangyari sa class.

[Rowan’s letter:25]

Kahit isang maliit na bagay lang.

[Rowan’s letter:26]

Gusto ko lang makarinig mula sa ’yo.

[Rowan’s letter:27]

Sabi ni Lola, kumain ka nang maayos at huwag masyadong magpuyat.

[Rowan’s letter:28]

Baka para sa ating dalawa ’yung last part.

[Rowan’s letter:29]

Ingat ka, okay?

[Rowan’s letter:30]

Ro

[Rowan’s letter:31]

P.S. Bangka pa rin ’yung drawing.
